Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any algebraic_topology module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 453. The projective resolution has length at most 317 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by algebraic_topology generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 453, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 317. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on algebraic_topology structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 452. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 819 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to algebraic_topology theory, we decompose the problem into 65 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 630. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
